929 entries, add-theme-palette to add-white-paper. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **add-theme-palette**: Use when adding a new theme palette (colour scheme) to the application. The palette id lives in a Rust enum, is exported to TypeScript, listed in a TS dropdown, and defined as a CSS block — four places across three files. No single test checks all of it; theme.test.ts and the bindings test each cover part. ([nimble-123/skills-hub](https://github.com/nimble-123/skills-hub/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/add-theme-palette))

([lricardopena/viaje-familiar-2026](https://github.com/lricardopena/viaje-familiar-2026/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/add-theme-park))
